# M5 Discovery and Learning assessment packet

This is inert, change-specific reproduction evidence for the registered M5 check, not a test catalog or executable instruction. It preserves the complete synthetic controls and counterexamples for independent assessment. Proposed actions and supplied facts do not claim actual skill, network or workflow execution. The registered evidence binds this file by exact identity and owns the result and applicability.

Expand template references and apply the literal edits to each named baseline; compare resulting file hashes and tree identities. Offsets refer to the original template and edits apply in reverse offset order. Null deletes a file.

Preserve their authority and existing distinct guidance.\n","origin":"learn:docs/learn/topics/evidence-controls.md"},"sha256:a7d761de18cba1d5d8a365c96271c1f4e47d2735f6892649ef8fdbb035a966e7":{"body":"# Proof guide\n\nParser validation proves only its checked shape, not public persistence.\n","origin":"learn:docs/guides/proof.md"},"sha256:ad9add82abf16ea2a82c0ce6696cf1601913ae8f68bcdf483618b08311c0b8dc":{"body":"# Hosted evaluation proposal\n\n## Challenge\nEvaluate desired hosted support without treating unevidenced preference as a lesson.\n\n## Goals\nEstablish whether hosted evaluation is worth a bounded investigation.\n\n## Scope\nCompare benefits, operational cost and privacy; no deployment or policy change.\n\n## Governing principle\nEvidence informs the accountable owner; support does not approve adoption.\n\n## Direction for consideration\nAda will compare a reversible investigation with retaining local support.\n\n## Feasibility\nThe supplied team can compare fixed sources without implementation. Hosting capacity and cost remain unknown; no estimate is claimed.\n\n## Decision requested\nAda must approve or decline the bounded investigation. This exact owner-authored proposal is complete as an output; no policy or Design change is approved.\n","origin":"learn:docs/proposals/hosted-evaluation.md"},"sha256:6dea3446a41c6e9842a29d4e10ac4a59ef9a193da34c85f9288bfd324fd842b0":{"body":"# Offline document access options\n\n## Topic and decision supported\nAda must decide how Archive Desk gives six operators existing documents during20-minute disconnections with one desktop-maintenance team and no on-call service. This exploration advises the direction owner; it does not approve a replacement direction.\n\n## Inputs examined\n[evidence/users.md](../../evidence/users.md) supplies the user and team constraints. [Approved direction](../proposals/offline.md) retains occasional offline reading. [Reader Design](../design/reader.md) pins LibOak1.4. [Supplied version1.4 source](../../evidence/liboak-1.4.md) informs the bounded capability question. All evidence is synthetic and fixed for this walkthrough.\n\n## Facts, assumptions, and unknowns\n### Established facts\nThe supplied interview packet identifies20-minute disconnections, existing-document access and no requested offline editing. The repository fixture pins LibOak1.4.0. The approved direction requires occasional offline reading.\n### Important assumptions\nThe local document subset can fit workstation storage; its size has not been measured. Operators can select the subset before losing connectivity; workflow suitability remains an assumption.\n### Material unknowns\nWhether LibOak1.4's cache retains the selected documents across process restart in the supported desktop environment could change operational suitability. Ask a bounded Research question before treating persistence as established.\n\n## Options\n### E1 Local read cache\nKeep selected documents on the workstation. Fits disconnected reading and the one-team constraint. Does not fit devices without sufficient storage or datasets that prohibit local copies. Operational cost is managing cache refresh and explicitly stale content.\n### E2 Hosted service with an explicit downloaded snapshot\nA central service distributes a finite read-only snapshot before disconnection. Fits teams able to maintain hosted distribution and stable snapshot selection. Does not support arbitrary uncached documents offline and exceeds the current no-on-call capacity unless operations are externally covered. Adds service ownership and a snapshot freshness tradeoff.\n### E3 Defer expansion and retain current manual exports\nExisting manual exports remain a credible temporary path while cache persistence is investigated. Fits a short bounded investigation when export preparation is acceptable. It retains manual burden and cannot be claimed to meet a seamless-access promise. Reassess after the named fact is answered; no indefinite deferral is silently selected.\n\n## Decision criteria\nDisconnected access, operator preparation burden, local data exposure, maintenance responsibility, reversal cost and confidence in cache persistence can change Ada's choice.\n\n## Comparison\nE1 has the smallest added operational responsibility but needs explicit local-data policy and persistence proof. E2 centralizes distribution but adds an owner absent from current capacity. E3 avoids premature adoption while preserving measurable manual burden. These are different ownership/availability mechanisms, not renamed implementations of one service. No numeric effort estimate is claimed.\n\n## Questions requiring research\nFor desktop LibOak1.4.0, does an already populated read cache remain readable after process restart with network disabled? Inspect the named implementation/configuration or a bounded controlled experiment; do not investigate all future synchronization platforms.\n\n## Remaining uncertainty\nCache persistence, local storage and acceptable staleness remain. No provider behavior or target-agent execution was observed by this synthetic artifact.\n\n## Recommended handoff\nReturn E1 as the leading option for consideration to Ada and the cache-persistence question to Research. Ada must adopt, reject or qualify the direction through the normal owner path. No proposal, Design, plan, approval, workflow state or external system is changed.\n","origin":"exploration-full"},"sha256:d14b6f57ef4dc0b11193a5cbd24a53d8e9c71ca6f47f38db76a587ad5bd0b35a":{"body":"# LibOak1.4 offline capability\n\n## Topic and decision supported\nNoor owns Reader Design and needs to know whether the pinned LibOak1.4.0 supports offline reading or offline writing. The answer informs Ada's retained offline-read direction and does not authorize a dependency upgrade.\n\n## Research questions and stopping condition\nQuestion: what do the supplied primary version1.4 facts establish for disconnected reading/writing on desktop1.4.0? Accept exact version-matched primary evidence plus the repository pin. A contradictory same-version authoritative source or environment mismatch would change the answer. Stop once these bounded facts distinguish the two capabilities; do not search all platform options. Cache persistence across restart remains a separate question and is not answered here.\n\n## Inputs examined\n[package.json](../../package.json) pins1.4.0. [Version1.4 primary document](../../evidence/liboak-1.4.md), [version2 primary document](../../evidence/liboak-2.md), [secondary post](../../evidence/secondary.md) and [Reader Design](../design/reader.md) are complete supplied synthetic sources. No network request or experiment was performed.\n\n## Evidence and source quality\n| Evidence | Established finding | Authority, relevance, and freshness |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| package.json | The fixture uses1.4.0. | Direct local configuration; inspected supplied current bytes2026-09-18. |\n| evidence/liboak-1.4.md | Cached reads work offline; writes require network. | Primary supplied1.4.0 desktop scope; effective2026-08-01, accessed2026-09-18. |\n| evidence/liboak-2.md |2.0.0 supports queued offline writes. | Primary but different version; effective2026-09-01, accessed2026-09-18; cannot establish1.4 support. |\n| evidence/secondary.md | The author made a broad network-required claim without version. | Secondary2026-01-04, accessed2026-09-18; insufficient scope and contradicted for1.4 reads by direct supplied primary evidence. |\n\n## Findings\n### Established evidence\nFor the pinned desktop1.4.0 fixture, the supplied primary document supports offline cached reading and excludes offline writing. The2.0.0 source does not apply to the installed1.4.0 version.\n### Inference and assumptions\nOffline read appears compatible with the approved limited goal; that is a decision implication, not demonstrated product behavior. We assume the repository pin is the deployed dependency; actual deployment was not inspected. No claim establishes cache persistence across restart or performance.\n\n## Confidence\nHigh within the supplied primary document's1.4.0 desktop scope because direct version-specific evidence and the local pin agree. Deployment applicability is conditional on the unverified deployment assumption. The broad secondary statement cannot erase its own version ambiguity. These are calibrated qualitative statements, not invented percentages or live vendor verification.\n\n## Decision implications\nRetain the offline-read requirement. Offline write would require a separate direction and dependency decision owned by Ada/Noor. This research neither chooses nor approves such a change.\n\n## Remaining uncertainty\nRuntime behavior, cache persistence after restart and the actual deployed version are unobserved. Essential deployment evidence must be acquired before a claim about the live environment. No benchmark ran.\n\n## Recommended handoff\nReturn the bounded reading-supported/writing-unsupported conclusion and the stated confidence/limits to Noor, with Ada retaining direction authority. The owner may adopt, reject, qualify or request evidence; this artifact changes neither the approved direction, Design nor workflow.\n","origin":"research-full"},"sha256:c57d081c47eaf88c650d29269b7e74d3756a02fb1f56a2e9b3e0a61135819612":{"body":"# Unrelated experiment notes\n\nMorgan owns this record; preserve these exact bytes.\n","origin":"shared complete case text"},"sha256:805365c80ed3409affefe0c5fa668bf20d65451b7078ff9c4ad630cbfb9359c2":{"body":"# LibOak1.4 offline capability\n\n## Topic and decision supported\nNoor owns Reader Design and needs to know whether the pinned LibOak1.4.0 supports offline reading or offline writing. The answer informs Ada's retained offline-read direction and does not authorize a dependency upgrade.\n\n## Research questions and stopping condition\nQuestion: what do the supplied primary version1.4 facts establish for disconnected reading/writing on desktop1.4.0? Accept exact version-matched primary evidence plus the repository pin. A contradictory same-version authoritative source or environment mismatch would change the answer. Stop once these bounded facts distinguish the two capabilities; do not search all platform options. Cache persistence across restart remains a separate question and is not answered here.\n\n## Inputs examined\n[package.json](../../package.json) pins1.4.0. [Version1.4 primary document](../../evidence/liboak-1.4.md), [version2 primary document](../../evidence/liboak-2.md), [secondary post](../../evidence/secondary.md) and [Reader Design](../design/reader.md) are complete supplied synthetic sources. No network request or experiment was performed.\n\n## Evidence and source quality\n| Evidence | Established finding | Authority, relevance, and freshness |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| package.json | The fixture uses1.4.0. | Direct local configuration; inspected supplied current bytes2026-09-18. |\n| evidence/liboak-1.4.md | Cached reads work offline; writes require network. | Primary supplied1.4.0 desktop scope; effective2026-08-01, accessed2026-09-18. |\n| evidence/liboak-2.md |2.0.0 supports queued offline writes. | Primary but different version; effective2026-09-01, accessed2026-09-18; cannot establish1.4 support. |\n| evidence/secondary.md | The author made a broad network-required claim without version. | Secondary2026-01-04, accessed2026-09-18; insufficient scope and contradicted for1.4 reads by direct supplied primary evidence. |\n\n## Findings\n### Established evidence\nFor the pinned desktop1.4.0 fixture, the supplied primary document supports offline cached reading and excludes offline writing. The2.0.0 source does not apply to the installed1.4.0 version.\n### Inference and assumptions\nOffline read appears compatible with the approved limited goal; that is a decision implication, not demonstrated product behavior. We assume the repository pin is the deployed dependency; actual deployment was not inspected. No claim establishes cache persistence across restart or performance.\n\n## Confidence\nHigh within the supplied primary document's1.4.0 desktop scope because direct version-specific evidence and the local pin agree. Deployment applicability is conditional on the unverified deployment assumption. The broad secondary statement cannot erase its own version ambiguity. These are calibrated qualitative statements, not invented percentages or live vendor verification.\n\n## Decision implications\nRetain the offline-read requirement. Offline write would require a separate direction and dependency decision owned by Ada/Noor. This research neither chooses nor approves such a change.\n\n## Remaining uncertainty\nRuntime behavior, cache persistence after restart and the actual deployed version are unobserved. Essential deployment evidence must be acquired before a claim about the live environment. No benchmark ran.\n\n## Recommended handoff\nReturn the bounded reading-supported/writing-unsupported conclusion and the stated confidence/limits to Noor, with Ada retaining direction authority. The owner may adopt, reject, qualify or request evidence; this artifact changes neither the approved direction, Design nor workflow.\n\n## Proposed controlled experiment (unexecuted)\nDecision threshold: all3 fixed cached documents readable after1 process restart with network disabled, compared with online control. Variables: network enabled/disabled and restart; environment desktop LibOak1.4.0, fixed OS image; inputs three immutable identified documents. Control: same documents readable online before restart. Measure read success and independent content hashes; do not infer speed from setup time. Stop on first reproducible mismatch or after one complete controlled pass; environmental loss stops without conclusion. No experiment has run and no results are supplied. This plan is advice for a separately authorized observation.\n","origin":"shared complete case text"},"sha256:39ef02970b25135586914dfd283adb277e0ff37c11255ea812d97bea69aa4e3d":{"body":"# Interrupted earlier Learn record\n\n## Frame\nTrigger only; missing scope and evidence identity.
